3. **Test all integration cards** — each must show a clear state (Connected / Not configured / Error) and a working "Test connection" action. For the M365 apps (Word/Excel/PowerPoint/OneNote) wire to the existing office/Graph adapters; if an adapter is stubbed, surface "Not configured" cleanly rather than a crash.

### REASONING
- Scattered connectors + messy views = looks unfinished, blocks demos and sales. A single clean Integrations tab is the surface we show prospects and sell the "Integrated edition" against. UI polish is now revenue-blocking, not cosmetic.

### EACH INTEGRATION CARD MUST HAVE
- Name + icon + one-line "what it does."
- Status badge: Connected (green) / Not configured (grey) / Error (red).
- **Test connection** button → calls the adapter's read-only health check, shows pass/fail inline.
- Edition gating: Azure AD, RSA admin, ServiceNow, CRM = **Integrated edition** only. Word/Excel/PPT/OneNote available where licensed. Standalone edition hides the gated cards cleanly.

### FILES LIKELY TO TOUCH (confirm in repo)
- `ARIA Sentinel/src/renderer/**` (new Integrations view/route + nav entry; restyle System/Settings views)
- `ARIA Sentinel/src/shared/directory.mjs`, `entra-graph-client.mjs`, `servicenow.mjs` (reuse health checks; add a uniform `testConnection()` per provider if missing)
- Office adapters (Word/Excel/PPT/OneNote) — locate or stub a `office-*.mjs` adapter with a read-only `testConnection()`
- Add `integrations.test.mjs` covering each card's status + testConnection paths

### SAFEST FIRST SLICE (ship this first, then iterate)
- Slice 1: add the **Integrations** nav tab + static cards (all 8) with status badges driven by existing health checks (ServiceNow/Entra/Directory wired; Office + RSA + CRM show "Not configured" stub). NO destructive changes. Land + show Ahmad a screenshot.
- Slice 2: wire **Test connection** to read-only checks per provider; add tests.
- Slice 3: UI cleanup pass on System Inventory + Settings (grouping, headers, kill the blank panel).

### APPROVAL GATES (STOP — do not do without Ahmad)
- No live writes to any directory/RSA/ServiceNow. Read-only health checks only.
- No new paid SDKs/APIs. No external sends. No publish to Netlify (Ahmad publishes Fri / on credit).
- Entra app registration + admin consent are Ahmad's tenant-admin actions — do NOT attempt.

### RISKS
- Office/Graph adapters may not exist yet → stub cleanly as "Not configured," don't fake "Connected."
- Don't regress the restored left-panel ARIA chat tab or the 3 modes / kill-switch.
- Watch the mount-truncation gremlin: build in /tmp clone, origin is truth, verify line counts after writes.

### NEXT PROMPT (Cowork will run after CC reports Slice 1)
"Slice 1 landed. Paste the Integrations tab screenshot + the new files list. Then do Slice 2 (Test connection wiring + integrations.test.mjs) and run the full test suite — report pass count."

### DEFINITION OF DONE
- One Integrations tab, 8 cards, status + Test connection each, edition-gated, tests green, UI no longer 'a mess', screenshots captured for the client setup guide + sales deck.

OBJECTIVE: Browser MVP of AXIS — the voice front-end to the director agent. Theme: cyan + black, calm breathing ring (per spec). Slices, screenshot each:

Recent coordination notes tail:
 words the KB never mentions) stay in the denominator → out-of-scope gets
     low coverage and is REJECTED (escalates).
  2. Heading/title boost — a query token hitting the doc's title or a ## heading (its real topic words)
     counts ×3, so "audio no sound" → Audio Issues, not the Windows blueprint. loadKbPack now folds all
     headings + a filename hint into the doc's title set.
  3. Explicit-platform-only bias — the HOST OS no longer biases routing; a blueprint is preferred ONLY when
     the user actually names that platform/device. (This was the main cause of OS-blueprint swallowing.)
  4. Light symmetric stemmer (crashing/crashes → crash) + curated synonyms (monitor→display, sound→audio,
     password→credential, charge→battery, mouse/keyboard→peripheral, …).
  Confidence floor MATCH_FLOOR 0.30; out-of-scope returns null (honest escalation).

Remaining 10 misroutes (reported honestly):
  • 3 in-scope, genuinely AMBIGUOUS — the diagnostic's own headings legitimately cover the symptom:
    bluetooth headphones→boot-issues, file-explorer→system-crashes, macbook-wake/sleep→display-issues
    (display has a "Power/sleep wake" cause). I did NOT bend the matcher to hit my own labels (HARD RULE 14).
  • 7 out-of-scope — all correctly ESCALATE (cisco/sap/kubernetes/forklift/payroll/salesforce/car); they
    matched a single generic word but count as escalations, so they do NOT pad the number.

## 2026-06-26 — AXIS COMMAND CENTER · ADDENDUM (Ahmad's refinements)

1) LEFT TABS = LIVE AGENT VIEW. Clicking a function (Send email, Find leads, Apply jobs, etc.) shows what that agent is doing in real time — its browser/app actions.
   HONEST v1: live step-log + periodic screenshot thumbnails captured from the agent's Claude-in-Chrome session (full-motion video = later upgrade). Label it "live view."
2) APPROVALS = unified INSTANT-approve inbox. Lists EVERY pending item incl. AXIS/Cowork asks. Each has Approve / Reject that executes IMMEDIATELY (writes the approval the worker/agent polls on a short interval → near-instant). One tap, it goes.
3) AUTO-APPROVE POLICY (default ON). Most email sends, job applies, and lead-draft sends AUTO-APPROVE within safe rails: approved template only · daily rate caps · known-good target filter · suppression list · anomaly check. ONLY escalate to Ahmad when truly irreversible / anomalous / no safe workaround → then it WAITS in Approvals. Post-hoc daily digest of everything that auto-fired so Ahmad can catch mistakes.
4) VOICE always-listening + BARGE-IN. Wake word "AXIS" armed continuously (NO mic press), speaker-locked to Ahmad only. Saying "AXIS" interrupts an in-progress action; then "don't send that — rewrite as X / use this template" redirects the agent live. "AXIS stop" halts.
5) Roster names already set (axis-roster.json). Name an agent to redirect it, or just say the intent and AXIS routes it.
6) Forge = Claude Code (the code builder).

GATES/HONESTY: auto-send carries real risk (CASL/spam, wrong applications) — rails above are mandatory, plus the daily auto-fire digest. Live "watch" v1 = log+screenshots, not video. No secrets in the public state JSON.

## 2026-06-26 — COWORK → CC: add SECURE in-app credentials form (Integrations tab) — URGENT for testing

PROBLEM: ARIA Sentinel reads DIRECTORY_TENANT_ID/CLIENT_ID/CLIENT_SECRET (and ServiceNow SN_*, DYNAMICS_URL) ONLY from a hidden .env.local in appRoot (main.mjs loadLocalEnv). For an installed app that's buried/asar-awkward and there is NO in-app field — Ahmad has nowhere to paste creds. Block: live AD/Entra + Dynamics + ServiceNow testing.

BUILD: in the Integrations tab, each card gets a "Configure" panel with secure inputs:
- Entra/AD card: Tenant ID, Client ID, Client Secret.
- Dynamics card: Dynamics/Dataverse URL (+ reuse Entra app).
- ServiceNow card: Instance URL, user, password (read-only integration user).
Store ENCRYPTED at rest via Electron safeStorage (NOT plaintext .env), in userData. On load, decrypt + feed into the same env the providers read (process.env or a config object passed to getStatus/testConnection). Secret fields masked, never logged, never sent anywhere except the provider's own GET. "Save" then "Test connection" → live read-only check flips the badge.
RULE 14 + privacy: secrets encrypted local-only, masked in UI, content-blind logs. Add tests (save→encrypt→load→provider reads; secret never in plaintext/logs). Build in /tmp clone, screenshot the Configure panel, report. This is also needed for every client, so build it cleanly.

Today's integrations are READ-ONLY. This adds GATED WRITES + the full case lifecycle. RULE 14 + all safety gates absolute. Sentinel is the connector (web hands off to Sentinel; Sentinel talks to ServiceNow + Entra).

PREREQS (Ahmad provides — flag if missing, NEVER fake):
- ServiceNow instance URL + an integration user with read+WRITE (itil / web_service role).
- Entra additional GATED WRITE scope for remediation + admin consent. HONEST NOTE: cloud Entra accounts aren't "locked" like on-prem AD — "unlock" maps to a password reset or `POST /users/{id}/revokeSignInSessions`. Build as a gated "remediate sign-in" action, labeled truthfully (not a fake "unlocked").
- A test user in the directory to remediate.

MODULE 1 — ServiceNow WRITE (ticket lifecycle): in servicenow.mjs add gated create Interaction → create Incident (linked) → update → resolve/close, via Table API (POST/PATCH). Behind the approval gate; read-back-verify the returned sys_id; audit each. Map: case → Interaction opens → Incident opens from interaction → on fix, Incident Resolved/Closed with close_notes, Interaction closed.

MODULE 2 — Entra gated remediation: in entra-graph-client/directory add `remediateUser` = password reset OR revokeSignInSessions, behind target-certainty (exact user or STOP) → validate → approve → execute → read-back GET to confirm → audit → rollback note. Never autonomous. Needs the write scope+consent; if absent → "Not authorized" (don't fake).

MODULE 3 — Case orchestration (the demo): web ARIA "Resolve it for me" → aria-sentinel:// handoff (exists) → Sentinel opens a case → ServiceNow Interaction+Incident (M1) → Entra remediation on the test user (M2, gated) → on success resolve+close incident → fire the session-end email (exists) with case summary + SLA. ServiceNow's own email-to-company = a NOTIFICATION rule Ahmad sets IN ServiceNow (incident created/resolved → email integrateditsupp@gmail.com) — document the steps; it's instance config, not Sentinel code.

MODULE 4 — Proactive silent resolution + user summary: Sentinel already detects local issues (clock drift etc.). For SAFE/low-risk issues, auto-resolve within the Confirmed/Autonomous gates (no user prompt), log a ServiceNow ticket per issue (M1), and on a cadence email the USER a summary: "ARIA resolved X + prevented Y so you weren't bothered" (session-report infra). REAL data only — list only what actually ran. NEVER auto-run risky/destructive fixes silently.

GATES: read-back-verify every write; never autonomous on a risky write; RULE 14 (no fake ticket #, no fake "unlocked"); secrets via safeStorage form; flag missing scope/instance instead of faking. /tmp clone, tests per module, screenshots, report. - **Secure "Configure" panels** on the Entra / CRM(Dynamics+HubSpot) / ServiceNow cards. Fields encrypted at
